# Conformance

Generated by `cargo xtask conformance`; never edit by hand. Each value of `oracle/expected/` (NCA against PKNCA), `oracle/expected/models/` (closed-form model values; `pk2/`: two-compartment values, partial derivatives and the inputs the engine must refuse) and `oracle/expected/fit/` (reference weighted least-squares fits) is compared with the engine within the tolerance named for its kind below; a value expected as not available must be not available. For the fits, the entries of the per-case tables are the groups of the oracle tests (estimates, residual sum of squares, statistics, confidence limits, information criteria, secondary parameters) and the convergence status. A value covered by a documented difference (`specs/differences.md`) is neither validated nor a failure: it has its own column and is left out of the totals. The floors (last lines: validated, expected and documented rows per parameter per case) can only go up.

**Overall: 32036 of 32036 values validated (100.0 %).** 16 further values are documented differences, neither validated nor failures.
